function helpView() {
  return `<div class="module-page help-manual-page"><div class="setting-card help-intro"><div><h3>UEP 사용 설명서</h3><p>각 메뉴의 자료 출처와 실제 업무 흐름을 기준으로 설명합니다.</p></div><span>${Object.keys(helpItems).length}개 메뉴</span></div><div class="help-grid">${Object.entries(helpItems).map(([key,item])=>`<article class="help-item"><button class="help-button" data-help="${key}"><span><b>${item.title}</b><small>${item.summary}</small></span><span>${helpOpen===key?"⌃":"⌄"}</span></button>${helpOpen===key?`<div class="help-content"><h4>${item.title}</h4><ol>${item.steps.map(step=>`<li>${step}</li>`).join("")}</ol><p><b>자료 출처:</b> ${item.source}</p><p><b>문제 확인 순서:</b> 원본시트 → 데이터처리시트 → UEP 화면 구현</p></div>`:""}</article>`).join("")}</div></div>`;
}

function classNumberOf(student) {
  return String(student?.className || "").replace(/[^0-9]/g, "");
}
function availableClasses() {
  const found = [...new Set((readonlyCache?.students || []).map(classNumberOf).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>Number(a)-Number(b));
  return found.length ? found : Array.from({length:9},(_,i)=>String(i+1));
}
function optionStudents(classNo, selectedId = "") {
  return (readonlyCache?.students || []).filter(s => !classNo || classNumberOf(s) === String(classNo)).map(s => `<option value="${escapeHtml(s.id)}" ${s.id === selectedId ? "selected" : ""}>${escapeHtml(s.studentNo)} $
()=>{button.disabled=true;button.textContent='진단 중…';const result=await window.schoolBoard?.previewReadonlySync?.().catch(()=>null);if(result?.ok){readonlyCache=result.data;googleConnectionError='';}else if(result?.reason){googleConnectionError=result.reason;}button.disabled=false;render('settings');toast(googleConnectionError?'연결 진단에서 확인할 항목이 있습니다.':'필수 연결이 정상입니다.');});
  $$('[data-access-control]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=()=>openAccessControlPanel());
  $$('[data-inline-access-refresh]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=async()=>{const result=await window.schoolBoard?.readReadonlyCache?.();if(result?.ok){readonlyCache=result.data;render('settings');toast('사용자 계정표를 새로고침했습니다.');}else toast(result?.reason||'사용자 계정표를 새로고침하지 못했습니다.');});
  $$('[data-inline-access-user]').forEach(row=>{const id=row.dataset.inlineAccessUser,account=(readonlyCache?.userAccounts||[]).find(a=>String(a.id)===String(id));if(!account)return;row.querySelector('[data-inline-access-toggle]')?.addEventListener('click',async()=>{const p=currentUserProfile();if(account.active!==false&&String(account.id)===String(p.id))return toast('현재 로그인한 관리자 계정은 자기 자신을 중지할 수 없습니다.');const active=account.active===false;const result=await window.schoolBoard?.updateUserAccount?.({id,active});if(!result?.ok)return toast(result?.reason||'사용자 승인 상태를 저장하지 못했습니다.');const refreshed=await window.schoolBoard?.readReadonlyCache?.();if(refreshed?.ok)readonlyCache=refreshed.data;render('settings');toast(active?'사용 승인을 완료했습니다.':'사용을 중지했습니다.');});row.querySelector('[data-inline-access-save]')?.addEventListener('click',async()=>{const role=row.querySelector('[data-inline-access-role]')?.value||'',grade=row.querySelector('[data-inline-access-grade]')?.value||'1',classNo=row.querySelector('[data-inline-access-class]')?.value||'';const admin=role==='관리자';const result=await window.schoolBoard?.updateUserAccount?.({id,role:admin?'관리자':role,admin,grade,classNo,active:account.active!==false});if(!result?.ok)return toast(result?.reason||'권한을 저장하지 못했습니다.');const refreshed=await window.schoolBoard?.readReadonlyCache?.();if(refreshed?.ok)readonlyCache=refreshed.data;render('settings');toast('사용자 권한을 저장했습니다.');});});
  $$('[data-check-update]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=async()=>{const url=$("#updateManifestUrl")?.value.trim()||state.settings.updateManifestUrl||"";if(!url)return toast("업데이트 정보 JSON 주소를 먼저 입력하세요.");button.disabled=true;button.textContent="확인 중…";const result=await window.schoolBoard?.checkUpdate?.(url,APP_VERSION);button.disabled=false;button.textContent="업데이트 확인";const box=$("#updateCheckResult");if(!result?.ok){if(box)box.innerHTML=`<b>확인 실패</b><small>${escapeHtml(result?.reason||'업데이트 정보를 읽지 못했습니다.')}</small>`;return;}if(box)box.innerHTML=result.updateAvailable?`<b>새 버전 ${escapeHtml(result.version)} 사용 가능</b><small>${escapeHtml(result.notes||'새 버전이 있습니다.')} · 확인경로 ${escapeHtml(result.source||'자동')}</small><button class="btn primary" data-apply-update>업데이트 적용</button>`:`<b>최신 버전입니다</b><small>현재 ${APP_VERSION} · 확인경로 ${escapeHtml(result.source||'자동')}</small>`;const apply=$("[data-apply-update]");if(apply)apply.onclick=async()=>{if(!result.sha256)return toast("업데이트 정보에 SHA-256이 없어 자동 적용할 수 없습니다.");if(!confirm(`UEP ${result.version} 업데이트를 적용할까요?\n\n현재 설정·인증·연결 정보는 그대로 유지됩니다. 적용 후 UEP가 자동으로 다시 실행됩니다.`))return;apply.disabled=true;apply.textContent="다운로드·검증 중…";const applied=await window.schoolBoard?.applyUpdate?.({version:result.version,downloadUrl:result.downloadUrl,sha256:result.sha256});if(!applied?.ok){apply.disabled=false;apply.textContent="업데이트 적용";return toast(applied?.reason||"업데이트 적용에 실패했습니다.");}toast("업데이트 검증 완료 · UEP를 재시작하여 적용합니다.");};});
  const timetableForm = $("#timetableNeisForm");
  if (timetableForm) timetableForm.onsubmit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    state.settings.neis.grade = $("#timetableGrade").value;
    state.settings.neis.classNo = $("#timetableClass").value;
    await save();
    loadNeisDashboard(true);
  };
  $$('[data-neis-clear-cache]').forEach((x) => (x.onclick = async () => {
    await window.schoolBoard?.clearNeisCache?.();
    neisData = null;
    neisError = "저장된 NEIS 자료를 지웠습니다.";
    render("settings");
  }));
  if ($("#studentTimetableSearchButton"))
    $("#studentTimetableSearchButton").onclick = searchStudentTimetable;
  if ($("#studentTimetableSearch"))
    $("#studentTimetableSearch").onkeydown = (event) => {
      if (event.key === "Enter") searchStudentTimetable();
    };
  if (page === "dashboard") bindQuickLinkSorting();
  if (page === "dashboard") updateDashboardTimetableControls();
  if (page === "dashboard") {
    const addButton = $("[data-dashboard-add]");
    const layoutButton = $("[data-dashboard-layout]");
    const managerButton = $("[data-widget-manager]");
    if (addButton) addButton.onclick = openModal;
    if (layoutButton) layoutButton.onclick = toggleDashboardEditing;
    if (managerButton) managerButton.onclick = openWidgetManager;
    bindDashboardLayoutEditing();
  }
  if (page === "students") {
    const input = $("#studentMasterSearch");
    const filter = $("#studentStatusFilter");
    const table = $("#studentMasterTable");
    const guide = $("#studentSelectionGuide");
    const selectionState = $("#studentSelectionState");
    let selectedClass = "";
    const runSearch = () => {
      const query = (input?.value || "").trim().toLowerCase();
      const status = filter?.value || "";
      const activeCriteria = Boolean(selectedClass || query || status);
      let visible = 0;
      $$("#studentMasterRows .student-row").forEach((row) => {
        const show = activeCriteria && (!selectedClass || row.dataset.class === selectedClass) && (!query || (row.dataset.search || "").includes(query)) && (!status || row.dataset.status === status);
        row.classList.toggle("hidden", !show);
        if (show) visible += 1;
      });
      table?.classList.toggle("hidden", !activeCriteria);
      guide?.classList.toggle("hidden", activeCriteria);
      $("#studentSearchEmpty")?.classList.toggle("hidden", !activeCriteria || visible > 0);
      if (selectionState) selectionState.textContent = selectedClass ? `${selectedClass}반 · ${visible}명` : query || status ? `검색 결과 ${visible}명` : "반을 선택하세요";
    };
    $$("[data-student-class]").forEach((button) => button.onclick = () => {
      const nextClass = button.dataset.studentClass || "";
      selectedClass = selectedClass === nextClass ? "" : nextClass;
      $$("[data-student-class]").forEach((item) => {
        const active = item.dataset.studentClass === selectedClass;
        item.classList.toggle("active", active);
        item.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(active));
      });
      runSearch();
    });
    if (input) input.oninput = runSearch;
    if (filter) filter.onchange = runSearch;
    runSearch();
  }
  if (page === "settings") updateConnectionControls();
  if (page === "forms") bindFormsPage();
  updateHeroClock();
  updateHeroWeather();
}

function bindFormsPage() {
  $("[data-draft-focus]").onclick = () => $("#draftAssistant").scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth" });
  $("[data-draft-load-program]")?.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    const id=$("#draftProgramSource")?.value||"";
    const p=(readonlyCache?.programs||[]).find(x=>String(x.id||x.programId||"")===String(id));
    if(!p)return toast("불러올 프로그램을 선택하세요.");
    const title=p.recordTitle||p.title||"프로그램";
    const participants=(p.students||[]).filter(x=>!/미참여|불참|취소/.test(String(x.result||x.status||""))).length;
    const period=[p.date,p.endDate].filter(Boolean).join(" ~ ")||p.date||"일정 확인";
    const place=p.place||p.location||"장소 확인";
    const target=p.target||p.targetText||(participants?`${participants}명`:"대상 확인");
    const memo=p.memo||p.description||p.purpose||"";
    if($("#draftTitle"))$("#draftTitle").value=`2026학년도 ${title} 운영 계획`;
    if($("#draftCategory"))$("#draftCategory").value=/진로|대학|전공/.test(title)?"진로·진학":"학년업무";
    if($("#draftBody"))$("#draftBody").value=`1. 관련: 2026학년도 학교교육계획\n2. 목적: ${memo||title+" 운영"}\n3. 운영 개요\n  가. 프로그램: ${title}\n  나. 운영기간: ${period}\n  다. 장소: ${place}\n  라. 대상: ${target}${participants?` (연결 참여 ${participants}명)`:""}\n4. 기타: UEP 연결자료를 불러온 뒤 세부사항을 확인하여 작성함.`;

  });
  $$('[data-template-preview]').forEach((b

  $("#drawerTitle")?.classList.remove("privacy-sensitive-text");
  $("#drawer")?.classList.remove("calendar-editor-drawer");
  clearInterval(studentPopupClockTimer);
  studentPopupClockTimer = null;
  $("#drawer").classList.add("hidden");
  $("#drawer").classList.remove("weather-expanded");
  $("#drawer").classList.remove("outing-expanded");
  $("#drawer").classList.remove("timetable-expanded");
  $("#drawer").classList.remove("student-expanded");
  $("#drawer").classList.remove("reference-expanded");
  $("#drawer").classList.remove("reference-maximized");
  $("#drawer").classList.remove("extension-expanded");
  $("#drawer").classList.remove("program-expanded");
  $("#drawer").classList.remove("duty-calendar-expanded");
  $("#drawer").classList.remove("dashboard-status-expanded");
  $("#drawer").classList.remove("dorm-deep-expanded");

  updateStudentSignalIndicator();
  $("#drawer").classList.remove("dorm-deep-wide");
  $("#drawerBackdrop").classList.add("hidden");
}

  // Dashboard safeguard: do not leave a holiday-time mode visibly selected on an ordinary weekday.
  // This only normalizes the selector when both labels exist; it does not remove holiday support.
  function normalizeHolidayMode(){
    const nodes=[...document.querySelectorAll('button,[role="button"],.chip,.pill,.seg-btn')];
    const holiday=nodes.find(el=>/공휴s*시간|공휴일/.test((el.textContent||'').trim()));
    const regular=nodes.find(el=>/정규s*시간/.test((el.textContent||'').trim()));
    if(!holiday||!regular) return;
    const now=new Date();
    const day=now.getDay();
    if(day===0||day===6) return;
    const hs=getComputedStyle(holiday);
    const rs=getComputedStyle(regular);
    const holidayLooksActive=holiday.getAttribute('aria-pressed')==='true'||holiday.classList.contains('active')||holiday.classList.contains('selected')||hs.backgroundColor!==rs.backgroundColor;
    if(holidayLooksActive && typeof regular.click==='function') regular.click();
  }
  setTimeout(normalizeHolidayMode,300);
  setTimeout(normalizeHolidayMode,1200);
})();

// 0.81.44: 상단 버전 배지에 새 업데이트 알림을 표시합니다. 재시작 자동 적용 동작은 그대로 유지합니다.
(()=>{
  const UPDATE_MANIFEST='https://raw.githubusercontent.com/hkhsm00-k81c/UEP-Updates/main/uep-version.json';
  const versionParts=v=>String(v||'').replace(/^v/i,'').split('.').map(n=>Number(n)||0);
  const newer=(a,b)=>{const x=versionParts(a),y=versionParts(b);for(let i=0;i<Math.max(x.length,y.length);i++){if((x[i]||0)!==(y[i]||0))return (x[i]||0)>(y[i]||0);}return false;};
  const decorate=latest=>{
    if(!newer(latest,APP_VERSION))return;
    [...document.querySelectorAll('button,span,div')].filter(el=>el.children.length===0&&el.textContent.trim()===`v${APP_VERSION}`).forEach(el=>{
      el.textContent=`v${APP_VERSION} · 업데이트 v${latest}`;
      el.title=`새 버전 v${latest}이 있습니다. UEP를 다시 시작하면 자동 적용됩니다.`;
      el.style.boxShadow='0 0 0 2px rgba(245,158,11,.45)';
      el.style.background='#fff3cd'; el.style.color='#8a5200';
    });
  };
  const check=async()=>{try{const r=await fetch(UPDATE_MANIFEST,{cache:'no-store'});if(!r.ok)return;const m=await r.json();decorate(m.version||m.latestVersion);}catch(_){}};
  addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>setTimeout(check,1200),{once:true});
  setInterval(check,10*60*1000);
})();


// __UEP_08166_CORE_RUNTIME__
function uepSharedSettingValue08166(key){
  const rows=Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.settings)?readonlyCache.settings:[];
  const hit=rows.find(r=>String(r?.key||r?.settingKey||r?.['설정키']||'').trim()===String(key));
  return String(hit?.value||hit?.settingValue||hit?.['설정값']||'').trim();
}
function uepUpsertLocalSharedSetting08166(key,value,description=''){
  if(!readonlyCache)readonlyCache={};
  if(!Array.isArray(readonlyCache.settings))readonlyCache.settings=[];
  const rows=readonlyCache.settings;
  const hit=rows.find(r=>String(r?.key||r?.settingKey||r?.['설정키']||'').trim()===String(key));
  const entry={key:String(key),value:String(value),description:String(description),settingKey:String(key),settingValue:String(value),'설정키':String(key),'설정값':String(value),'설명':String(description)};
  if(hit)Object.assign(hit,entry);else rows.push(entry);
}
async function uepSaveSharedSecurity08166(key,value,description){
  if(!window.schoolBoard?.saveSharedSecurityConfig)throw new Error('공용 보안설정 저장 기능을 사용할 수 없습니다.');
  const result=await window.schoolBoard.saveSharedSecurityConfig({key,value,description,editor:currentLoginTeacherName?.()||''});
  if(!result?.ok)throw new Error(result?.reason||'공용 보안설정을 저장하지 못했습니다.');
  uepUpsertLocalSharedSetting08166(key,value,description);
  return true;
}

// 민감정보: 관리자/학년부장이 공용 시트에 설정, 담임은 동일 비밀번호 입력 후 열람.
const __uepSensitiveSecurityConfig08166=sensitiveSecurityConfig;
sensitiveSecurityConfig=function(){
  const shared=uepSharedSettingValue08166('SENSITIVE_PIN_HASH');
  if(shared)return {passwordHash:shared,shared:true};
  return __uepSensitiveSecurityConfig08166();
};
sensitivePasswordConfigured=function(){return !!sensitiveSecurityConfig().passwordHash;};
setSensitivePasswordFlow=async function(){
  if(!canConfigureSensitivePassword())return toast('관리자·학년부장만 민감정보 비밀번호를 설정·변경할 수 있습니다.');
  const configured=sensitivePasswordConfigured();
  const result=await sensitivePasswordModal({mode:'set',configured});if(!result)return;
  if(configured&&await sensitiveHash(result.current)!==sensitiveSecurityConfig().passwordHash)return toast('현재 비밀번호가 일치하지 않습니다.');
  const passwordHash=await sensitiveHash(result.first);
  try{
    await uepSaveSharedSecurity08166('SENSITIVE_PIN_HASH',passwordHash,'민감정보 열람 공용 비밀번호 SHA-256 해시');
    localStorage.setItem(UEP_SENSITIVE_SECURITY_KEY,JSON.stringify({passwordHash,updatedAt:new Date().toISOString(),migrated:true}));
    lockSensitiveSession();toast('민감정보 공용 비밀번호를 저장했습니다.');if(state.activePage==='settings')render('settings');
  }catch(error){toast(error?.message||'민감정보 공용 비밀번호 저장 실패');}
};

// 선택과목 대외비: 공용 시트에 단일 해시를 저장하여 모든 담임이 같은 비밀번호로 인증.
subjectConfidentialSharedHash=function(){return uepSharedSettingValue08166('SUBJECT_CONFIDENTIAL_PIN_HASH');};
subjectConfidentialPasswordHash=function(){return subjectConfidentialSharedHash()||String(localStorage.getItem(UEP_SUBJECT_CONFIDENTIAL_PIN_KEY)||'');};
subjectConfidentialPasswordConfigured=function(){return !!subjectConfidentialPasswordHash();};
setSubjectConfidentialPassword=async function(){
  if(!subjectConfidentialAllowed()){toast('관리자·학년부장 권한에서 설정할 수 있습니다.');return false;}
  const configured=subjectConfidentialPasswordConfigured();
  const result=await sensitivePasswordModal({mode:'set',configured});if(!result)return false;
  if(configured&&await subjectConfidentialDigest(result.current)!==subjectConfidentialPasswordHash()){toast('현재 선택과목 비밀번호가 일치하지 않습니다.');return false;}
  const passwordHash=await subjectConfidentialDigest(result.first);
  try{
    await uepSaveSharedSecurity08166('SUBJECT_CONFIDENTIAL_PIN_HASH',passwordHash,'선택과목 대외비 공용 비밀번호 SHA-256 해시');

  }catch(error){toast(error?.message||'선택과목 공용 비밀번호 저장 실패');return false;}
};

// 기존 관리자 PC 로컬값이 있고 공용값이 비어 있으면 관리자 로그인에서 한 번만 중앙으로 승격.
async function uepMigrateLocalSecurity08166(){
  if(!(typeof canConfigureSensitivePassword==='function'&&canConfigureSensitivePassword()))return;
  try{
    const localSensitive=(()=>{try{return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(UEP_SENSITIVE_SECURITY_KEY)||'{}')?.passwordHash||'';}catch{return '';}})();
    if(localSensitive&&!uepSharedSettingValue08166('SENSITIVE_PIN_HASH'))await uepSaveSharedSecurity08166('SENSITIVE_PIN_HASH',localSensitive,'민감정보 열람 공용 비밀번호 SHA-256 해시');
    const localSubject=String(localStorage.getItem(UEP_SUBJECT_CONFIDENTIAL_PIN_KEY)||'');
    if(localSubject&&!uepSharedSettingValue08166('SUBJECT_CONFIDENTIAL_PIN_HASH'))await uepSaveSharedSecurity08166('SUBJECT_CONFIDENTIAL_PIN_HASH',localSubject,'선택과목 대외비 공용 비밀번호 SHA-256 해시');
  }catch(error){console.warn('[UEP security migration]',error);}
}

  if(!quiet&&typeof toast==='function')toast(result?.reason||'자동실행 설정을 저장하지 못했습니다.');return false;
}

// 0.81.44: 상단 버전 배지에 새 업데이트 알림을 표시합니다. 재시작 자동 적용 동작은 그대로 유지합니다.
(()=>{
  const UPDATE_MANIFEST='https://raw.githubusercontent.com/hkhsm00-k81c/UEP-Updates/main/uep-version.json';
  const versionParts=v=>String(v||'').replace(/^v/i,'').split('.').map(n=>Number(n)||0);
  const newer=(a,b)=>{const x=versionParts(a),y=versionParts(b);for(let i=0;i<Math.max(x.length,y.length);i++){if((x[i]||0)!==(y[i]||0))return (x[i]||0)>(y[i]||0);}return false;};
  const decorate=latest=>{
    if(!newer(latest,APP_VERSION))return;
    [...document.querySelectorAll('button,span,div')].filter(el=>el.children.length===0&&el.textContent.trim()===`v${APP_VERSION}`).forEach(el=>{
      el.textContent=`v${APP_VERSION} · 업데이트 v${latest}`;
      el.title=`새 버전 v${latest}이 있습니다. UEP를 다시 시작하면 자동 적용됩니다.`;
      el.style.boxShadow='0 0 0 2px rgba(245,158,11,.45)';
      el.style.background='#fff3cd'; el.style.color='#8a5200';
    });
  };
  const check=async()=>{try{const r=await fetch(UPDATE_MANIFEST,{cache:'no-store'});if(!r.ok)return;const m=await r.json();decorate(m.version||m.latestVersion);}catch(_){}};
  addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>setTimeout(check,1200),{once:true});
  setInterval(check,10*60*1000);
})();


// __UEP_08166_CORE_RUNTIME__
function uepSharedSettingValue08166(key){
  const rows=Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.settings)?readonlyCache.settings:[];
  const hit=rows.find(r=>String(r?.key||r?.settingKey||r?.['설정키']||'').trim()===String(key));
  return String(hit?.value||hit?.settingValue||hit?.['설정값']||'').trim();
}
function uepUpsertLocalSharedSetting08166(key,value,description=''){
  if(!readonlyCache)readonlyCache={};
  if(!Array.isArray(readonlyCache.settings))readonlyCache.settings=[];
  const rows=readonlyCache.settings;
  const hit=rows.find(r=>String(r?.key||r?.settingKey||r?.['설정키']||'').trim()===String(key));
  const entry={key:String(key),value:String(value),description:String(description),settingKey:String(key),settingValue:String(value),'설정키':String(key),'설정값':String(value),'설명':String(description)};
  if(hit)Object.assign(hit,entry);else rows.push(entry);
}
async function uepSaveSharedSecurity08166(key,value,description){
  if(!window.schoolBoard?.saveSharedSecurityConfig)throw new Error('공용 보안설정 저장 기능을 사용할 수 없습니다.');
  const result=await window.schoolBoard.saveSharedSecurityConfig({key,value,description,editor:currentLoginTeacherName?.()||''});
  if(!result?.ok)throw new Error(result?.reason||'공용 보안설정을 저장하지 못했습니다.');
  uepUpsertLocalSharedSetting08166(key,value,description);
  return true;
}

// 민감정보: 관리자/학년부장이 공용 시트에 설정, 담임은 동일 비밀번호 입력 후 열람.
const __uepSensitiveSecurityConfig08166=sensitiveSecurityConfig;
sensitiveSecurityConfig=function(){
  const shared=uepSharedSettingValue08166('SENSITIVE_PIN_HASH');
  if(shared)return {passwordHash:shared,shared:true};
  return __uepSensitiveSecurityConfig08166();
};
sensitivePasswordConfigured=function(){return !!sensitiveSecurityConfig().passwordHash;};
setSensitivePasswordFlow=async function(){
  if(!canConfigureSensitivePassword())return toast('관리자·학년부장만 민감정보 비밀번호를 설정·변경할 수 있습니다.');
  const configured=sensitivePasswordConfigured();
  const result=await sensitivePasswordModal({mode:'set',configured});if(!result)return;
  if(configured&&await sensitiveHash(result.current)!==sensitiveSecurityConfig().passwordHash)return toast('현재 비밀번호가 일치하지 않습니다.');
  const passwordHash=await sensitiveHash(result.first);
  try{
    await uepSaveSharedSecurity08166('SENSITIVE_PIN_HASH',passwordHash,'민감정보 열람 공용 비밀번호 SHA-256 해시');
    localStorage.setItem(UEP_SENSITIVE_SECURITY_KEY,JSON.stringify({passwordHash,updatedAt:new Date().toISOString(),migrated:true}));
    lockSensitiveSession();toast('민감정보 공용 비밀번호를 저장했습니다.');if(state.activePage==='settings')render('settings');
  }catch(error){toast(error?.message||'민감정보 공용 비밀번호 저장 실패');}
};

// 선택과목 대외비: 공용 시트에 단일 해시를 저장하여 모든 담임이 같은 비밀번호로 인증.
subjectConfidentialSharedHash=function(){return uepSharedSettingValue08166('SUBJECT_CONFIDENTIAL_PIN_HASH');};
subjectConfidentialPasswordHash=function(){return subjectConfidentialSharedHash()||String(localStorage.getItem(UEP_SUBJECT_CONFIDENTIAL_PIN_KEY)||'');};
subjectConfidentialPasswordConfigured=function(){return !!subjectConfidentialPasswordHash();};
setSubjectConfidentialPassword=async function(){
  if(!subjectConfidentialAllowed()){toast('관리자·학년부장 권한에서 설정할 수 있습니다.');return false;}
  const configured=subjectConfidentialPasswordConfigured();
  const result=await sensitivePasswordModal({mode:'set',configured});if(!result)return false;
  if(configured&&await subjectConfidentialDigest(result.current)!==subjectConfidentialPasswordHash()){toast('현재 선택과목 비밀번호가 일치하지 않습니다.');return false;}
  const passwordHash=await subjectConfidentialDigest(result.first);
  try{
    await uepSaveSharedSecurity08166('SUBJECT_CONFIDENTIAL_PIN_HASH',passwordHash,'선택과목 대외비 공용 비밀번호 SHA-256 해시');
    localStorage.setItem(UEP_SUBJECT_CONFIDENTIAL_PIN_KEY,passwordHash);lockSubjectConfidential();toast('선택과목 공용 비밀번호를 저장했습니다.');if(state.activePage==='settings')render('settings');return true;
  }catch(error){toast(error?.message||'선택과목 공용 비밀번호 저장 실패');return false;}
};

// 기존 관리자 PC 로컬값이 있고 공용값이 비어 있으면 관리자 로그인에서 한 번만 중앙으로 승격.
async function uepMigrateLocalSecurity08166(){
  if(!(typeof canConfigureSensitivePassword==='function'&&canConfigureSensitivePassword()))return;
  try{
    const localSensitive=(()=>{try{return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(UEP_SENSITIVE_SECURITY_KEY)||'{}')?.passwordHash||'';}catch{return '';}})();
    if(localSensitive&&!uepSharedSettingValue08166('SENSITIVE_PIN_HASH'))await uepSaveSharedSecurity08166('SENSITIVE_PIN_HASH',localSensitive,'민감정보 열람 공용 비밀번호 SHA-256 해시');
    const localSubject=String(localStorage.getItem(UEP_SUBJECT_CONFIDENTIAL_PIN_KEY)||'');
    if(localSubject&&!uepSharedSettingValue08166('SUBJECT_CONFIDENTIAL_PIN_HASH'))await uepSaveSharedSecurity08166('SUBJECT_CONFIDENTIAL_PIN_HASH',localSubject,'선택과목 대외비 공용 비밀번호 SHA-256 해시');
  }catch(error){console.warn('[UEP security migration]',error);}
}

// Windows 시작 시 자동실행: 최초 0.81.66 적용 시 기본 ON, 사용자가 설정에서 끌 수 있음.
const UEP_AUTOSTART_PREF_08166='uep_windows_autostart_v1';
function uepAutoStartEnabled08166(){return localStorage.getItem(UEP_AUTOSTART_PREF_08166)!=='0';}
async function uepApplyAutoStart08166(enabled,quiet=false){
  if(!window.schoolBoard?.setAutoStart)return false;
  const result=await window.schoolBoard.setAutoStart(Boolean(enabled));
  if(result?.ok){localStorage.setItem(UEP_AUTOSTART_PREF_08166,enabled?'1':'0');if(!quiet&&typeof toast==='function')toast(enabled?'Windows 시작 시 UEP가 자동 실행됩니다.':'Windows 자동 실행을 해제했습니다.');return true;}
  if(!quiet&&typeof toast==='function')toast(result?.reason||'자동실행 설정을 저장하지 못했습니다.');return false;
}
async function uepInitAutoStart08166(){
  if(!window.schoolBoard?.setAutoStart)return;
  if(localStorage.getItem(UEP_AUTOSTART_PREF_08166)===null)await uepApplyAutoStart08166(true,true);
}

// 설정 > 시스템 관리 화면에 자동실행 카드 삽입.
const __uepSettingsView08166=settingsView;
settingsView=function(){
  let 
  const next=ev.target&&ev.target.closest?ev.target.closest('[data-uep-student-next]'):null;
    if(prev||next){
      ev.preventDefault();
      ev.stopImmediatePropagation();
      const ids=visibleStudentIds();
      if(!ids.length) return;
      let cur='';
      try{ cur=String(recordStudentId||'').trim(); }catch(_e){}
      let i=ids.indexOf(cur);
      if(i<0) i=0;
      i=prev?Math.max(0,i-1):Math.min(ids.length-1,i+1);
      try{ recordStudentId=ids[i]; }catch(_e){}
      renderRecordsSafe();
    }
  },true);

  // Dashboard safeguard: do not leave a holiday-time mode visibly selected on an ordinary weekday.
  // This only normalizes the selector when both labels exist; it does not remove holiday support.
  function normalizeHolidayMode(){
    const nodes=[...document.querySelectorAll('button,[role="button"],.chip,.pill,.seg-btn')];
    const holiday=nodes.find(el=>/공휴s*시간|공휴일/.test((el.textContent||'').trim()));
    const regular=nodes.find(el=>/정규s*시간/.test((el.textContent||'').trim()));
    if(!holiday||!regular) return;
    const now=new Date();
    const day=now.getDay();
    if(day===0||day===6) return;
    const hs=getComputedStyle(holiday);
    const rs=getComputedStyle(regular);
    const holidayLooksActive=holiday.getAttribute('aria-pressed')==='true'||holiday.classList.contains('active')||holiday.classList.contains('selected')||hs.backgroundColor!==rs.backgroundColor;
    if(holidayLooksActive && typeof regular.click==='function') regular.click();
  }
  setTimeout(normalizeHolidayMode,300);
  setTimeout(normalizeHolidayMode,1200);
})();

settingsView=function(){
  let html=__uepSettingsView08166();
  if(settingsPanel==='s
